- **yomiyasu-mini**: 試験用の小さな一式。生成の道具の試験だけに使い、配らない。 ([nanaism/yomiyasu-mini](https://github.com/nanaism/yomiyasu-mini/tree/HEAD/tests/fixtures/basic/expected/skills/yomiyasu-mini) · ★24)
- **yomiyasu-switch**: Connect to unchanged upstream yomiyasu and manage its application to Japanese answers in the current conversation. Do not change state from quotations, design discussion, or a request that merely happens to be written in Japanese. ([yusukefujiijp/ai-project](https://github.com/yusukefujiijp/ai-project/tree/HEAD/skills/yomiyasu-switch))
- **yomu**: 資料 (README・設計資料・手順書・SKILL.md など) が「深く考えなくても理解できる」かを、軽量モデル (Haiku) にブラインドで読ませて検証するスキル。Haiku に資料本文だけを渡して要点を自分の言葉で再現させ、書き手の意図とのズレから分かりにくい箇所を特定する。/haiku:yomu と入力されたとき、「Haiku にレビューさせて」「この資料分かりやすい?」「軽量モデルで読解チェックして」と頼まれたとき、または資料の分かりやすさを客観的に確かめたいときに使う。日本語で書かれた資料に使う (英語の資料には haiku:retell を使う)。コードの正しさのレビューには使わない。 ([simiraaaa/haiku-yomu](https://github.com/simiraaaa/haiku-yomu/tree/HEAD/skills/yomu))
- **yon-read**: Read, interpret, or explain existing YON content. Trigger on any .yon file, YON snippet (lines with @TAG|...|... patterns), or when the user says "read this YON", "what does this YON mean", "explain this @DOC/@RULE/@STEP", "summarize this YON", "interpret this record". Do NOT trigger for generic format questions ("what is YON?") with no YON content present — and do not trigger when the user wants to *write* YON (use yon-write). ([allemaar/open-skills](https://github.com/allemaar/open-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/yon-read) · ★15)
- **yon-write**: YON Writing & Compilation Mode. Use when creating, generating, converting, or writing YON content. Triggers on: "write YON", "convert to YON", "create a .yon file", "encode this as YON", "YON format", or any request where the expected output is YON records. Also triggers when unstructured content should be structured into YON even without explicit keyword — e.g., working in a YON project. Use yon-read instead when reading, interpreting, explaining, or summarizing existing YON. ([allemaar/open-skills](https://github.com/allemaar/open-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/yon-write) · ★15)
